Extensive experiments demonstrate that SWIM significantly outperforms baselines in listwise reranking tasks, yielding substantial improvements in overall recommendation engagement.

</details>


### [176] [The "Curse of Knowledge" in LLM Query Simulation: Concept Provenance for Tracing Answer-Side Intrusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25245)
*Chenglong Ma,Xinye Wanyan,Danula Hettiachchi,Ziqi Xu,Jeffrey Chan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出概念溯源框架，用于检测大语言模型生成检索查询中的答案侧知识入侵问题。实验发现此类入侵普遍存在（7.4%非通用概念，覆盖97%主题），主要通过知识入侵和部署入侵两种机制发生。虽然局部检索效应显著，但仅解释<2%整体评估方差。提示工程无法消除入侵，但生成后基于概念溯源的筛选可实现99%清除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成的查询广泛用于信息检索评估增强，但可能包含预设答案侧文档知识的概念，违反了预搜索用户的信息获取边界。现有验证指标（重叠度、多样性、有效性）无法区分罕见的人类长尾变体与候选答案侧入侵。

Method: 引入概念溯源框架，将查询概念分配至四个区域：背景支持区、人类核心区、人类长尾区、候选答案侧区。在100个UQV100主题、8个LLM、5种提示条件下，通过两条抽取管道处理77,004个查询。进行人工验证（68.2%宽松精确率）和诊断探针实验测量局部检索效应。

Result: 跨管道token-HCIR斯皮尔曼ρ达1.0。候选答案侧概念占非通用概念7.40%，出现在97/100主题中，主题解释约67%方差。发现两大入侵机制：知识入侵45.5%、部署入侵45.0%。删除效应量d=-0.47（随机删除d=-0.34），但仅解释<2%聚合评估方差。无提示条件能消除入侵，生成后概念溯源选择实现99%清除。

Conclusion: 概念溯源作为边界合规诊断工具而非评估偏移预测器。在测试条件下，提示工程无法消除答案侧知识入侵，但基于概念溯源的生成后筛选可有效解决（99%清除率），为IR评估中的LLM查询生成提供了实用的质量控制手段。

Abstract: LLM-generated search queries are widely used to augment IR evaluation, yet they may contain concepts that presuppose answer-side document knowledge, violating the information-access boundary of pre-search users. Existing validation metrics, including overlap, diversity, and effectiveness, cannot distinguish rare human-tail variation from candidate answer-side intrusion. We introduce concept provenance, a framework that assigns query concepts to backstory-supported, human-central, human-tail, and candidate answer-side zones, operationalizing a boundary that retrieval metrics alone cannot detect. Applying concept provenance to 77,004 queries across 100 UQV100 topics, 8 LLMs, and 5 prompt conditions with two extraction pipelines, we obtain a cross-pipeline token-HCIR Spearman rho of 1.0 over five condition means. Candidate answer-side concepts constitute 7.40 percent of non-generic concepts and appear in 97 of 100 topics, with topic explaining approximately 67 percent of variance. Human validation yields 68.2 percent relaxed precision, revealing two mechanisms: knowledge intrusion at 45.5 percent and deployment intrusion at 45.0 percent. Diagnostic probes show disproportionate localized retrieval effects, with deletion effect size d = -0.47 compared with d = -0.34 for random deletion, but these concepts explain less than 2 percent of aggregate evaluation variance. Concept provenance therefore serves as a boundary-compliance diagnostic rather than an evaluation-shift predictor. Under the tested conditions, no prompt condition eliminates intrusion; post-generation concept-provenance selection achieves 99 percent elimination.

</details>


### [177] [Rank-Deviation Quality: A Distance-Aware Metric for Multi-Answer Retrieval and Ranking Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25318)
*Xiaokun Zhou,Alessandro Moschitti,Danielle Class*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种新的检索和排序系统评估指标——Rank-Deviation Quality (RDQ)，该指标适应不同数量参考项的查询，结合输出位置权重和秩偏差惩罚，同时考虑检索项的覆盖率和顺序一致性，在多个基准测试中表现出更高的统计效力和更快的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估指标（如NDCG、Kendall's tau）要么需要绝对相关性等级，要么只关注顺序相关性而忽略检索项的覆盖率，且难以适应不同应用场景下的可见性权重和对误序的容忍度。需要一种基于有序参考列表（ORL）的指标，能够同时评估“检到了哪些项”以及“项的排序是否正确”，并支持序数标注和可调节的误序容忍参数。

Method: RDQ 以有序参考列表（ORL）为基准，对候选排名中的每个参考项给予：输出位置权重 × 秩偏差惩罚；不在 ORL 中的项得零分。输出位置权重可映射界面可见性（如垂直列表、轮播图）；秩偏差惩罚由应用特定参数控制，较大值强调召回有效项，较小值强调顺序匹配。RDQ 仅需序数标注（成对或列表判断），无需绝对相关性等级。

Result: 在 5000 查询 POI 数据集（12 个系统）上，RDQ 在 13 种指标配置中拥有最高的中位数经验功效@100；RDQ 在 200 查询时达到与自身全查询排序的 mean tau ≥ 0.8 一致，而最强非 RDQ 配置 RBP(0.9) 需 250 查询。在 TREC Deep Learning 基准上，RDQ 使用从分级标签导出的序数层级，在 n=25 时达到与 NDCG 相当的中位数功效，NDCG 在 n=100 时更高。

Conclusion: RDQ 是一种灵活、基于序数的排序评估指标，能同时兼顾检索覆盖率和排序质量，适应不同界面可见性和误序容忍需求。实验表明其统计效力更强、收敛更快，适合大规模检索系统的离线评估与在线 A/B 测试指标设计。

Abstract: We introduce Rank-Deviation Quality (RDQ), an evaluation metric for retrieval and ranking systems that adapts to queries with varying numbers of reference items, from a single correct answer to many valid results. RDQ scores a candidate ranking against an ordered reference list (ORL): each retrieved reference item contributes its output-position weight multiplied by a rank-deviation penalty, and items outside the ORL receive zero credit. Application-specific parameters control tolerance to misordering. Larger values emphasize retrieving valid reference items, whereas smaller values place more weight on matching their reference order. The output-position weights can reflect visibility in the application's interface, such as a vertical list or a carousel. Unlike metrics that require absolute relevance grades, RDQ operates on ordinal rankings, which annotators can produce through pairwise or listwise judgments. Unlike rank-correlation measures such as Kendall's tau, RDQ accounts for both which items are returned and how they are ordered. On a 5,000-query point-of-interest (POI) dataset with 12 systems, RDQ has the highest median empirical power@100 among the 13 evaluated metric configurations. It reaches mean tau >= 0.8 agreement with its own full-query ordering at 200 queries; RBP(0.9), the strongest tested non-RDQ configuration, reaches the same threshold at 250. On TREC Deep Learning benchmarks, where NDCG uses native graded labels and RDQ uses ordinal tiers derived from them, RDQ reaches comparable median power at n=25, while NDCG is higher at n=100.

</details>


### [178] [CRAMER: Control via Request-Aware Masking for Editing Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25370)
*Zhiyuan Julian Su,Naihe Feng,Zhen Luther Qin,Ga Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 CRAMER 框架，利用用户自然语言请求作为控制信号，通过掩码机制冻结骨干网络参数实现即时适应，无需重新训练或依赖大语言模型推理，在多个大规模基准数据集上超越现有方法，开创请求感知序列推荐新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐模型难以灵活响应用户即时请求，现有适配方法要么需重新训练整个骨干网络，要么依赖大语言模型推理（提示工程），计算开销大，难以应用于大规模推荐服务。

Method: 受模型控制理论启发，将用户自然语言请求视为控制信号，通过请求感知掩码机制调制冻结的骨干网络参数，实现对多样化请求的即时适应，避免昂贵的重新训练。

Result: 在多个大规模基准数据集上，CRAMER 在多个推荐指标上优于四个最先进的请求感知基线，同时实现极小开销，并展现出更强的可控性和跨域适应性。

Conclusion: CRAMER 确立了请求感知序列推荐的新范式，通过控制理论视角实现高效、即时的用户请求适配，为大规模推荐服务提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation models, while powerful, have limited flexibility in responding to immediate user requests, making it difficult to adapt their recommendations to the user's timely interests. Unfortunately, existing user request adaptation methods often incur high computational overhead due to either 1) retraining the entire backbone network or 2) leveraging the inference ability of large language models (a.k.a. prompt engineering), limiting their applicability in large-scale recommendation services. This paper presents Control via Request-Aware Masking for Editing Recommenders (CRAMER), a framework that takes users' natural-language requests to immediately change sequential recommendation models' behavior. Specifically, inspired by the model control theory, CRAMER treats user requests as control signals to modulate frozen backbone parameters through masking, achieving instant adaptation to diverse requests while avoiding costly retraining. Experiments on multiple large-scale benchmark datasets show that CRAMER outperforms four state-of-the-art request-aware baselines across multiple recommendation metrics while achieving minimal overhead. Moreover, the proposed framework exhibits enhanced controllability and cross-domain adaptability, establishing a new paradigm for request-aware sequential recommendation.

</details>


### [179] [MOTIF: Motivation-guided Topology Inference for Cold-start Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25381)
*Yurui Shi,Yuchen Miao,Ximing Hu,Zijun Wang,Chang Han*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 MOTIF 框架，通过动机引导的拓扑推理解决冷启动多模态推荐中的三大挑战：交互稀疏、冷门项拓扑孤立、相似度图语义漂移。结合离线 LLM 推理、知识增强图重构、加权图对比学习和语义-结构对齐，在三个基准上显著超越现有基线，最高相对提升 6.07%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷启动多模态推荐面临三大耦合难题：(i) 稀疏交互导致用户意图难以捕捉；(ii) 冷门项在图中拓扑隔离，缺乏连接；(iii) 基于相似度构建的项图易引入语义漂移，降低推荐质量。现有方法未能同时解决这三个问题。

Method: 提出 MOTIF（Motivation-guided Topology Inference Framework），包含四大模块：1) 语义动机推理：利用离线 LLM 推理用户隐性动机语义；2) 知识增强图重构：融合外部知识重构可迁移的项-项拓扑；3) 加权图对比学习：学习鲁棒图嵌入，增强拓扑与语义一致性；4) 语义-结构对齐：对齐语义空间与图结构空间，避免生成文本直接注入预测。

Result: 在三个多模态推荐基准上实验，MOTIF 持续优于基于图、多模态、冷启动及 LLM 增强等各类基线，相对最强近期基线最高提升 6.07%。

Conclusion: MOTIF 通过动机引导的拓扑推理，有效缓解冷启动多模态推荐中的稀疏交互、拓扑孤立和语义漂移问题，展示了离线 LLM 推理与知识增强图学习结合的优势，为冷启动推荐提供了新范式。

Abstract: Cold-start multimodal recommendation faces three coupled challenges: (i) sparse interactions obscure user intent, (ii) cold items remain topologically isolated, and (iii) similarity-based item graphs may cause semantic drift. To address these issues, we propose MOTIF, a Motivation-guided Topology Inference framework for cold-start multimodal recommendation. MOTIF integrates Semantic Motivation Reasoning, Knowledge-enhanced Graph Reconstruction, Weighted Graph Contrastive Learning, and Semantic-Structural Alignment. It uses offline LLM reasoning to infer motivation semantics, reconstructs transferable item-item topology, and learns robust graph embeddings without injecting generated text into prediction. Experiments on three multimodal benchmarks show consistent gains over graph-based, multimodal, cold-start, and LLM-enhanced baselines, with up to 6.07% relative improvement over the strongest recent baseline.

</details>


### [180] [DocPC: Document-Level Visual Retrieval via Representative Page Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25434)
*Chengsong You,Junwei Zhou,Nan Du*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 DocPC，一种基于代表性页面组合的文档级视觉检索框架，通过选取代表性页面并拼接为单一网格图像实现文档级索引，大幅降低存储与索引开销，并结合多正样本对比学习与稀疏调度列表式优化处理文档级多正样本监督，在新基准 DocViRe 上取得 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉文档检索方法以页面为中心，与现实中需要完整文档检索的场景不匹配；朴素的页面聚合文档策略存在线性索引成本高、跨页面相关性导致检索效果下降等问题。

Method: 1. 代表性页面组合：选取文档的代表性页面并拼接为单一网格图像，实现文档级索引，减少索引图像、向量及存储 10.1 倍，端到端索引时间约 7.7 倍。2. 多正样本对比学习与稀疏调度列表式优化相结合，处理文档级普遍存在的多正样本监督问题。3. 构建 DocViRe 基准，包含多正样本相关性标注。

Result: DocPC-ColQwen 在 DocViRe 上 NDCG@5 达到 44.09，优于最强页面级基线 38.91，同时存储减少 10.1 倍。

Conclusion: DocPC 通过代表性页面组合实现高效文档级视觉检索，显著降低存储与计算开销，并针对多正样本监督设计优化策略，在新基准上建立了新的 SOTA，为大规模视觉文档检索提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Visual document retrieval has advanced by encoding page screenshots with vision-language models, bypassing OCR pipelines. However, existing methods remain page-centric, misaligned with real-world scenarios requiring complete document retrieval. A naive page-then-document aggregation suffers from linear indexing cost and degraded retrieval when relevance spans multiple pages. We propose DocPC, a document-level visual retrieval framework based on Representative Page Composition: selecting representative pages and composing them into a single grid image for document-level indexing, reducing indexed images, vectors, and storage by 10.1x and end-to-end indexing time by roughly 7.7x. To handle multi-positive supervision prevalent at the document level, we combine multi-positive contrastive learning with sparsely scheduled listwise optimization. We also introduce DocViRe, a benchmark with multi-positive relevance annotations. DocPC-ColQwen achieves NDCG@5 of 44.09 on DocViRe, outperforming the strongest page-level baseline at 38.91 while reducing storage by 10.1x. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DocPC-Document-Level-Visual-Retrieval-via-Representative-Page-Composition-1D52. Data is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/anonymous-7219/docpc.

</details>


### [181] [Query Expansion Is More Than Generation: Improving Dense Retrieval through Better Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25521)
*Siyuan Sun,Mihai Surdeanu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 AnchorQE，一种无需训练的查询扩展集成方法，通过分别编码原始查询和扩展并插值，使用无监督在线策略估计插值因子，显著提升密集检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作关注生成什么扩展文本，但忽略了如何将生成文本整合到密集检索器中；研究发现性能下降常归因于整合方法本身，而非扩展质量。

Method: AnchorQE 将原始查询和扩展分别编码，然后插值；插值因子通过无监督在线策略估计，仅在扩展既具检索强又与原始查询检索证据一致时给予高信任。

Result: 在 TREC-DL、LoTTE 和 BEIR 上，AnchorQE 相比扩展-only 和文本级拼接基线提升高达 12.89%；在线策略估计插值因子比开发集调优的固定权重高出 3.81%。

Conclusion: 查询扩展的整合方式至关重要，AnchorQE 通过分离编码和自适应插值有效解决了扩展引入的噪声问题，无需训练即可显著提升密集检索性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate query expansions without task-specific training, yet the same expansions often make a frozen dense retriever worse. We identify an underexplored factor: prior work has often focused on what text is generated, while how generated text is incorporated into dense retrievers has received less systematic attention. By holding generated expansions fixed, we show that performance degradation can often be attributed to the integration method itself. We introduce AnchorQE, a training-free method that separately encodes the original query and its expansion before interpolating them. The interpolation factor is estimated using an unsupervised online strategy that operates over a small part of the unlabeled test stream. Intuitively, our strategy assigns high expansion trust only when expansions are both retrieval-strong and consistent with the original query's retrieved evidence. We show that AnchorQE improves retrieval effectiveness by up to 12.89% when compared to widely-used expansion-only or text-level concatenation baselines across TREC-DL, LoTTE, and BEIR. Further, we show that our online strategy to estimate the interpolation factor outperforms a fixed weight tuned on a development partition by up to 3.81%.

</details>


### [182] [TransRetrieval: Scaling Up Transformer-Based Retrieval for Industrial Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25528)
*Zhifei Zheng,Yunfei Liu,Bin Liu,Qiren Zhu,Hanbing Liu,Ziru Xu,Han Zhu,Jian Xu,Qi Qi,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 TransRetrieval，一种基于 Transformer 的推荐检索框架，通过加权平均聚合、目标 Token 压缩和位置风格域嵌入解决特征异质性导致的 Token 范数发散问题，实现了计算预算与跨域数据的联合扩展，在工业数据集和公开基准上验证了对数线性缩放规律，并在线上 A/B 测试中提升平台收入 2.53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐检索中应用缩放定律受阻于特征异质性：直接堆叠 Transformer 层因异质字段导致严重的 Token 范数发散，收益递减。需要一种能同时随计算预算和跨域数据扩展的 Transformer 检索框架。

Method: 提出 TransRetrieval，包含三大核心组件：(1) 加权平均聚合，恢复 Transformer 依赖的同质 Token 假设；(2) 目标 Token 压缩，将每候选 FLOPs 降低 85% 且保持交叉注意力表达力；(3) 位置风格域嵌入，以极低额外成本统一多域数据，使跨域数据成为扩展资产。

Result: 在 400 亿交互工业数据集和 KuaiRand 基准上，将每目标计算从 0.1 扩展到 2 MFLOPs，Recall@2000 分别提升 19.3/22.2 个百分点，验证了稳健的对数线性缩放规律。在线 A/B 测试中，在相同端到端延迟约束下，平台收入提升 2.53%。

Conclusion: TransRetrieval 通过解决 Token 范数发散、降低计算开销和统一跨域表示，成功将 Transformer 缩放定律引入推荐检索，实现了计算与数据的双重扩展，显著提升了离线指标和在线业务收益，为大规模推荐检索提供了可落地的扩展范式。

Abstract: Applying scaling laws to recommendation retrieval is hindered by feature heterogeneity: naively stacking Transformer layers yields diminishing returns because heterogeneous fields produce severe token-norm divergence. We present TransRetrieval, a Transformer-based retrieval framework that scales with both computational budget and cross-domain data. The key enabler is (1) weighted average aggregation, which restores the homogeneous-token assumption Transformers rely on. Building on this, we introduce (2) target token compression that cuts per-candidate FLOPs by 85% while preserving cross-attention expressiveness, and (3) position-style domain embeddings that unify multiple domains at negligible additional cost, turning cross-domain data into a scaling asset. On a 40-billion-interaction industrial dataset and the public KuaiRand benchmark, scaling compute from 0.1 to 2 MFLOPs per target yields +19.3/+22.2 pt Recall@2000, confirming robust log-linear scaling. In online A/B tests, TransRetrieval lifts platform revenue by 2.53% under the same end-to-end latency constraint as the production baseline.

</details>


### [183] [An Event is Worth One Token: Event Tokenization for Industrial-scale LLM Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25546)
*Fan Xia,Zhaoheng Zheng,Iman Setayesh,Ruogu Lin,Yiqin Pan,Samarth Mittal,Wentao Bao,Vinti Pandey,Sachin Patil,Jianpeng Cheng,Jun Xiao,Zhuang Wang,Xiangjun Fan,Sri Reddy,Minghai Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出AMBER框架，通过将每个交互事件压缩为紧凑的Event Token，引入快照分辨率作为新的缩放维度，显著提升LLM推荐系统的性能，并在工业级基准上取得最优计算-质量权衡，同时展现出跨实体类型和模型架构的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推荐系统在序列建模中，每个位置仅编码文本、语义ID或少量分类特征，丢弃了用户、物品、上下文及结果等丰富信号，导致每个位置的查询较弱，且劣化在自回归建模中跨序列累积。

Method: 提出以事件为中心的范式，用完整时间快照表示每次交互；引入快照分辨率作为新缩放维度；设计AMBER将快照压缩为Event Token（新LLM输入模态），端到端学习表示，并预计算缓存Event Token以解耦服务时计算开销。

Result: 在工业级排序和检索基准上，AMBER推进了计算-质量帕累托前沿；足够容量下，统一Tokenizer优于专用实体Tokenizer，显示跨结构不同实体类型的正向迁移；Event Token集成到非LLM排序器作为历史特征，也带来统计显著提升；进一步扩大Tokenizer容量持续改进。

Conclusion: AMBER通过Event Token高效缩放快照分辨率，解决了自回归推荐中信息丢失和累积劣化问题，实现了更优的推荐质量与计算效率，且具备跨实体类型和模型架构的泛化迁移能力。

Abstract: LLM-based recommendation has scaled along model capacity and sequence length, yet each position encodes only text, semantic IDs, or a few categorical features, discarding rich user, item, context, and outcome signals available at each event. Under autoregressive modeling, this yields weak queries at each position and, since each position becomes context for the next, the degradation compounds across the sequence. We propose an event-centric paradigm that represents each interaction by its full temporal snapshot, and identify a new scaling dimension we term snapshot resolution: the amount of information encoded per event. To efficiently scale snapshot resolution, we introduce AMBER (Autoregressive Modeling via Bottlenecked Event Representation), which compresses each temporal snapshot into a compact Event Token, a new LLM input modality. The representation is learned end-to-end, while Event Tokens are pre-computed and cached for serving, decoupling snapshot resolution from real-time serving compute. On industrial-scale ranking and retrieval benchmarks, AMBER advances the compute-quality Pareto frontier relative to alternative recommendation paradigms. At sufficient capacity, a single unified tokenizer even outperforms dedicated per-entity tokenizers, demonstrating positive transfer across structurally different entity types. AMBER's Event Tokens also transfer across model architectures: when integrated into a heavily optimized non-LLM ranker as serving-time historical features, they yield statistically significant improvements. Further scaling Event Tokenizer capacity provides additional improvements.

</details>


### [184] [When Stale Constraints Go Unchecked: Budgeted Verification Failures in Inherited Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25553)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究探讨智能体在有限验证预算下如何处理继承记忆中的过时约束，实验表明将验证槽位重新分配至关键溯源路径可大幅提升决策与当前记录的一致性，揭示验证分配是导致陈旧记忆错误的主要因素，记忆系统需要独立的新鲜度或超越信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体继承巩固记忆时可能包含已被权威记录撤销的过时约束。在验证预算稀缺的情况下，智能体能否恢复撤销信息？若不能，错误是否可在不增加预算的情况下避免？

Method: 显式建模超越关系，历史溯源不可变，仅当前记录变化。固定验证预算为两条记录，对比智能体原生分配与将一个槽位重新分配至关键溯源路径或随机记录的策略。在多个运行（主实验、重述复现、保留域）和六个模型中评估。

Result: 原生分配下，智能体仅约20%的情节检查溯源路径；约束被超越时，原生分配导致77.3%、74.7%、74.7%的陈旧一致决策。将一个槽位重新分配至关键路径，使当前记录一致决策提升+74.0、+72.7、+61.3个百分点，六模型全正向；记录与记忆一致时无变化。保留域发现时间不一致，修正后复现得+73.3点。干预利用关键路径知识，非调度器，表明陈旧记忆错误中归因于验证分配的比例接近结构上限。

Conclusion: 记忆系统可能需要独立于相关性之外的新鲜度或超越信号，以在有限验证预算下避免陈旧记忆错误。

Abstract: An agent that inherits a consolidated memory may inherit a constraint that was true when written and has since been withdrawn by a newer authoritative record. Under a scarce verification budget, does the agent recover the withdrawal, and if not, is the error avoidable without spending more? We model supersession explicitly -- historical provenance is immutable; what changes is which record is current -- and assign by design the memory's form, the world's state (source current or superseded), and the verification policy at a fixed budget of two records: the agent's own allocation, or the same budget with one slot re-assigned to the critical provenance path or to a random record. With a constraint stated, agents inspected its provenance path in about one episode in five; when that constraint had been superseded, native allocation produced stale-consistent decisions in 77.3%, 74.7% and 74.7% of episodes across a primary run, a fresh-wording replication and a held-out domain. Re-assigning one slot to the critical path raised current-record-consistent decisions by +74.0, +72.7 and +61.3 points, positive in six of six models in each of those runs, and changed nothing when the record agreed with the memory. The held-out scenario was later found to contain a temporal inconsistency; a robustness replication with one sentence corrected, deposited externally before execution, gave +73.3 points and is reported alongside the original. The intervention uses knowledge of the critical path and is not a scheduler; it identifies that the share of stale-memory error attributable to verification allocation is close to its structural ceiling. Memory systems may need freshness or supersession signals separate from relevance.

</details>


### [185] [RetrievalRouter: Joint Modality and Architecture Selection for Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25625)
*Emre Kuru,Mehmet Onur Keskin,Reza Farahbakhsh,Noel Crespi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 RetrievalRouter，一种轻量级查询感知路由器，根据查询文本动态选择最佳检索管道，在准确率和延迟之间实现更优权衡，超越静态基线和自适应方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档检索管道在模态（文本或多模态）和架构（稠密或后交互）上差异大，导致准确率与延迟的硬性妥协：高效管道太慢昂贵，快速管道无法从复杂文档检索证据。从业者必须在漏证据和不可用延迟间二选一，缺乏查询级自适应选择依据。

Method: 提出 RetrievalRouter，轻量级查询感知路由器，仅从查询文本学习选择最适合的检索管道。单一可调参数暴露完整准确率-延迟前沿，为每个静态基线提供同时更准确更快的操作点。

Result: 对比最佳静态基线，RetrievalRouter 准确率提升 2.5%，速度快 12.4 倍。相比先前自适应策略选择方法，在准确率导向设置下 nDCG@5 显著更高，在延迟导向设置下 nDCG@5 和延迟均匹配或数值上更优。

Conclusion: 查询级自适应管道选择可消除准确率-延迟妥协，RetrievalRouter 实现了这一目标，代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Document retrieval increasingly supports high-stakes information access in finance, healthcare, and law. Modern retrieval pipelines vary both in modality (text or multimodal) and in retrieval architecture (dense or late-interaction). These choices impose a hard compromise: the most effective pipelines are too slow and expensive to run at scale, while the fastest fail to retrieve evidence from complex documents. Practitioners must therefore choose between missed evidence and unusable latency, with no principled basis for adapting that choice at the query level. We show that this compromise is unnecessary. Not every query requires the same pipeline. Across benchmarks spanning financial and scientific corpora, no static pipeline dominates. We introduce RetrievalRouter, a lightweight query-aware router that learns, from the query text alone, which retrieval pipeline best fits each query. A single tunable parameter exposes the full accuracy-latency frontier, and for every static baseline, RetrievalRouter offers an operating point that is simultaneously more accurate and faster. Against the best static baseline, RetrievalRouter is 2.5% more accurate and 12.4 times faster. Furthermore, compared with prior adaptive strategy selection methods, RetrievalRouter achieves significantly higher nDCG@5 across accuracy-oriented settings, while matching or numerically outperforming them on both nDCG@5 and latency in latency-oriented settings. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/emrekuruu/retrieval-router.

</details>


### [186] [Data Citation for Large Language Models: A Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25663)
*Gianmaria Silvello*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文指出大语言模型（LLM）的数据引用是一个与文档级引用不同且更难的开放性挑战，学术引用除验证外还服务于信用分配与溯源功能，并适用于数据与文本。文章提出三大研究方向：训练数据归因、推理时数据引用、知识图谱事实引用，强调需数据库、信息检索、知识表示与AI社区跨学科合作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将引用视为文本文档的验证手段，但学术引用还承担信用分配与溯源功能，且适用于数据。LLM日益成为信息获取的中介，亟需解决如何对数据进行引用，使输出可验证、溯源可追踪、数据创作者与策展人获得应有信用。

Method: 文章未提出具体技术方法，而是界定问题空间并设定三大研究方向：1) 训练数据归因——将影响力估计转化为被吸收进模型参数的语料的参考文献；2) 推理时数据引用——在恰当粒度与固定性上识别数据集、子集与查询结果；3) 知识图谱事实引用——定义对单个三元组的引用含义及信用如何沿溯源链传播。

Result: 明确了数据引用区别于文档级引用 grounding 的独特挑战，梳理出三个正交且相互依赖的研究方向，指出解决这些问题需要数据库、信息检索、知识表示与人工智能四个社区的联合攻关。

Conclusion: 大语言模型的数据引用是一个跨学科的开放性难题，需从训练时归因、推理时引用、知识图谱事实引用三个维度系统推进，依赖数据库、IR、KR 与 AI 社区的协作创新，以实现可验证、可溯源、信用公平的数据引用生态。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly mediate access to information, and a growing body of work asks whether they cite the sources behind their outputs. That work treats citation as a verification device and applies it to textual documents. Scholarly citation serves two further functions, credit and provenance, and it applies to data as much as to text. This paper argues that data citation for large language models is an open challenge, distinct from document-level citation grounding and harder to solve. We ask how such models should cite data so that outputs stay verifiable, provenance stays traceable, and credit reaches data creators and curators. We set out three research directions. Training data attribution has to turn influence estimates into references for corpora absorbed into model parameters. Data citation at inference time has to identify datasets, subsets, and query results at the right granularity and fixity. Citing knowledge graph facts has to define what a reference to a single triple denotes and how credit propagates along provenance. Progress on all three depends on joint work across the database, information retrieval, knowledge representation, and artificial intelligence communities.

</details>


### [187] [D3ER: Supporting Multi-Modal Recommendation via Disentangle and Distillation-based Dynamic Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25737)
*Bingnan Wang,Yi Li,Xiongxin Tang,Fanjiang Xu,Jiangmeng Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为D3ER的多模态推荐方法，首次引入梯度提升框架交替学习模态同质性判别信息(HOI)和模态异质性判别信息(HEI)，并结合知识蒸馏与全局修正正则化解决存储开销和局部最优问题，在真实数据集上验证了优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态推荐方法联合学习模态同质性判别信息(HOI)和模态异质性判别信息(HEI)，导致两者相互干扰、削弱各自效果。需要一种能够解耦并专门优化这两类信息的方法。

Method: 提出D3ER框架：1) 引入梯度提升机制，形式化交替学习HOI和HEI的优化目标，使各专用模型聚焦擅长样本；2) 引入知识蒸馏压缩模型集合，降低存储成本；3) 设计全局修正正则化缓解梯度提升易陷入局部最优的问题。

Result: 在主流真实世界数据集上进行实验，D3ER在多模态推荐任务上优于现有基线方法，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过梯度提升实现HOI与HEI的解耦交替学习，配合知识蒸馏和全局修正正则化，D3ER有效解决了多模态推荐中同质性与异质性信息联合学习相互干扰的问题，显著提升了推荐性能。

Abstract: Incorporating items' information shared among multiple modalities into a fused representation, multi-modal recommendation (MR) has demonstrated documented success than canonical unimodal recommendation. Although several attempts have been made to extract the discriminative information unique in each modality, existing methods suffer from a core limitation: the joint learning of modal-homogeneity discriminative information (HOI) and modal-heterogeneity discriminative information (HEI) tends to weaken their individual effectiveness. To remedy this deficiency, we propose a novel method, dubbed Disentangle and Distillation-based Dynamic Ensemble for multi-modal Recommendation (D3ER). We introduce gradient boosting into MR for the first time to formalize the optimization objective for alternately learning HOI and HEI. This design enables models dedicated to each type of information to focus on their proficient samples, thereby promoting specialized optimization. Furthermore, to mitigate the inherent high storage cost and risk of local optima in gradient boosting, we enhance our framework with knowledge distillation and a global correction regularization. Experiments on prevalent real-world datasets confirm the superiority of our proposed method on MR.

</details>


### [188] [Hamiltonian Spectral-Temporal Dissipative Dynamics for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25755)
*Shuiying Liao,P. Y. Mok*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Hamiltonian Spectral Recommender (HSR)，将序列推荐建模为二阶耗散哈密顿系统，在频域求解并引入可学习耗散与局部冲击模块，捕捉用户偏好的惯性、周期性与突变，实验显著优于现有 Transformer 和 SSM 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐模型多假设偏好演化为一阶过程，仅依赖当前潜在表示，难以捕捉真实用户行为中的惯性、周期性和突然偏移等高阶动态特征。

Method: 引入二阶动力学视角，构建耗散哈密顿系统，将偏好分解为位置（稳定偏好）与动量（短期趋势）；利用线性时不变方程在频域获得闭式解；设计可学习耗散机制建模兴趣自然衰减，并引入局部冲击细化模块处理稀疏日志中的突变行为。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上，HSR 持续超越最先进的基于 Transformer 和状态空间模型（SSM）的推荐器。

Conclusion: HSR 通过哈密顿相空间建模，联合捕获全局周期模式、惯性演化和局部冲击，填补了现有序列模型对这三类现象表达不足的空白。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation requires understanding how user preferences evolve over time, yet most existing models treat such evolution as a first order process where the next state depends solely on the current latent representation. Nevertheless, real user behavior often exhibits richer dynamics, including inertia, periodicity, and sudden shifts that cannot be fully captured by these first order assumptions. Motivated by these behavioral characteristics, we reconceptualize sequential recommendation through the lens of second order dynamical systems and introduce the Hamiltonian Spectral Recommender (HSR), which recasts preference evolution as a dissipative Hamiltonian system in a latent phase space of position (stable preference) and momentum (short-term tendency). The linear time-invariant structure of the governing equation admits a closed-form solution in the frequency domain. A learnable dissipation mechanism further captures natural interest decay, while a short local impulse refinement module models abrupt behavioral fluctuations commonly observed in sparse interaction logs. This design jointly accounts for global periodic patterns, inertial evolution, and localized shocks, where three phenomena that are underrepresented in existing sequential models. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that HSR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art Transformer-based and state space model (SSM)-based recommenders.

</details>


### [189] [PUMA: Post-Hoc Sparsification of Universal Multimodal Embeddings for Efficient Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25780)
*Matteo Attimonelli,Alessandro De Bellis,Franco Maria Nardini,Claudio Pomo,Cosimo Rulli,Rossano Venturini,Tommaso Di Noia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PUMA 是一种稀疏自编码器方法，将通用多模态嵌入映射为紧凑稀疏编码，无需重新训练主干网络。通过预训练保持稠密点积几何，再微调稀疏编码器用于检索。在五个基准测试中，PUMA 在四个数据集上达到或超越稠密检索性能，存储减少 8-16 倍，推理加速高达 25 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多模态嵌入器支持文本、图像及组合查询检索，但其稠密表示带来高内存和推理开销。事后稀疏化虽可降低成本，但在多模态检索中研究尚浅。

Method: PUMA 采用两阶段稀疏自编码器：1) 预训练阶段保持稠密点积几何结构；2) 微调阶段针对检索任务优化稀疏编码器。整个过程冻结主干网络，仅训练稀疏化模块。

Result: 在 Qwen3-VL-Embedding-2B 上评测五个基准（文本到图像、组合图像检索），四个数据集性能统计上不劣于或优于稠密检索。存储压缩 8-16 倍（FP32），大候选池下推理加速高达 25 倍。同时揭示两种失效模式：预 TopK 支持不足、检索错位的激活支持。

Conclusion: PUMA 证明了事后稀疏化在多模态检索中的有效性，以极小性能损失换取显著的存储和推理加速，为高效多模态检索提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Universal multimodal embedders enable retrieval across text, image, and combined queries, but their dense representations incur high memory and inference costs. Post-hoc sparsification could reduce these costs but remains underexplored for multimodal retrieval. We introduce PUMA, a sparse autoencoder recipe that maps universal multimodal embeddings to compact sparse codes without retraining the backbone: a pretraining stage preserves dense dot-product geometry, after which the sparse encoder is fine-tuned for retrieval. We evaluate on five benchmarks covering text-to-image and composed image retrieval. On Qwen3-VL-Embedding-2B, PUMA is statistically indistinguishable from or improves over dense retrieval on four of five datasets. We further identify two failure modes of post-hoc sparsification: insufficient pre-TopK support and retrieval-misaligned active support. PUMA reduces vector storage by 8-16x (FP32) and is up to 25x faster than exact dense scoring on larger candidate pools, enabling efficient multimodal retrieval.

</details>


### [190] [PlanSightRAG: A Visual-First Multimodal RAG for Automating Question Answering and Compliance Checking for Civil Standard Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26091)
*Nabaraj Subedi,Shuvo Dip Datta,Ahmed Abdelaty,Shivanand Venkanna Sheshappanavar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 PlanSightRAG，一个面向土木基础设施合规性检查的视觉优先多模态 RAG 框架，直接对工程图纸图像进行索引与推理，解决了传统 OCR 方法丢失几何与布局信息的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 土木基础设施合规性检查长期依赖工程师人工阅读传统 2D 图纸；基于 OCR 的自动化方法剥离了理解图纸所必需的几何与布局信息，导致检查准确性受限。

Method: 构建 PlanSightRAG 框架：采用 ColNomic-3B 多向量检索对图纸图像直接编码；设计 Agentic Planner-Retriever-Auditor-Synthesizer 多智能体流程进行推理；引入 MaxSim 热力图作为证据溯源。并构建包含 5 个州 DOT 标准图纸共 4,056 对、1,898 页的基准数据集。

Result: 零样本检索 Recall@5 达 91.47%，在保留的 Michigan DOT 语料上为 91.40%。在合成参数化合规图纸上，Qwen2.5-VL-72B 流水线在提供预解析规则阈值时达到 100% 判决准确率，而非 VLM OCR 基线仅 76.4%。还演示了从规范语料中自主提取数值限值的视觉规则落地能力，无需人工提供规则。

Conclusion: PlanSightRAG 通过视觉优先的多模态 RAG 实现了高精度的工程图纸合规性自动化检查，显著优于基于 OCR 的基线，并具备从规范文本自主落地规则的能力，为土木工程数字化合规检查提供了可落地的技术路径。

Abstract: Civil infrastructure compliance checking has long relied on engineers manually reading legacy 2D plans; however, OCR-based automation strips away the geometry and layout essential for interpreting these plans. We present a Visual-First Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) framework called PlanSightRAG. It indexes and reasons directly over plan imagery, integrates a ColNomic-3B multi-vector retrieval, an agentic Planner-Retriever-Auditor-Synthesizer, and MaxSim heatmaps as an evidence trail. We introduce a 4,056-pair benchmark from five state Departments of Transportation (DOT) standard plans (1,898 pages). PlanSightRAG achieves 91.47% Recall@5 on zero-shot retrieval, while on a held-out Michigan DOT corpus, it achieves 91.40%. On synthetic, parametrically-generated compliance drawings, our Qwen2.5-VL-72B pipeline reaches 100% verdict accuracy only when supplied a pre-resolved rule threshold, a controlled ceiling that a non-VLM OCR baseline already reaches at 76.4%. Finally, we demonstrate autonomous visual rule-grounding by extracting numeric limits directly from a specification corpus without any human-supplied rules.

</details>


### [191] [VisDocAgentBench: Benchmarking Agents for Visually Rich Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17889)
*Lexiang Hu,Yanzhao Zhang,Mingxin Li,Dingkun Long,Yikang Li,Fuwei Zhang,Yisen Wang,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Visually rich documents encode relevance through language, layout, structured visual elements, and corpus context, yet retrieval is typically evaluated by one-shot query--page matching. Agentic-search benchmarks usually score downstream question answering or report generation, leaving document ranking under iterative evidence acquisition underexplored. We introduce VisDocAgentBench, a closed-corpus benchmark comparing static and agentic retrieval under a shared ranked-output contract. It contains 2,375 pages from 100 documents and 120 unique-target queries balanced across direct, one-bridge, and two-bridge evidence structures. Relation-preserving construction yields semantic, relational, and visual queries, followed by full-document review and hard-negative validation. A strong late-interaction visual retriever reaches 97.50% Recall@1 on direct items but 2.50% on two-bridge items, exposing the limits of query--target matching when relevance depends on corpus context. Agents recover much of this loss, but planner choice and retrieval representation remain decisive. Every planner performs better with visual retrieval, whose best R@1 reaches 67.50% versus 37.50% for OCR-text. Ablations identify iterative search and page inspection as consequential capabilities, and providing the complete support context improves ranking on both routes. Trace analysis localizes the remaining losses to target discovery, candidate examination, and evidence-role integration. These findings motivate retrieval agents that combine modality-preserving discovery with evidence-directed verification.

</details>

### [192] [VLM-based automatic multi-granularity graph representation of building layouts for design informatics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24886)
*Song Guo,Zhuoshi Chen,Maosu Li,Weimin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多粒度图层级（LoGs）及基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的自动构建方法，实现公共建筑平面图的免标注、可扩展结构化图表示提取，在147个学术图书馆平面图上验证：中粒度图在节点级区域预测最优（Macro F1=0.647），粗粒度图在图级布局质量评估最优（Spearman ρ=0.610）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑平面图蕴含丰富的功能空间关系知识，支撑设计检索、知识推理和BIM全生命周期丰富，但自动构建面向任务的自适应图表示仍具挑战性。

Method: 定义多粒度Level-of-Graphs (LoGs)；设计基于VLM的自动LoG构建流程：节点识别、边推理、文本解析、图粗化；以147个全球学术图书馆平面图为案例研究进行系统评估。

Result: VLM生成的图与人工标注高度一致（节点匹配率≥92%，每平面图三层LoG生成耗时509.3秒）；中粒度图在节点级区域预测最佳（Macro F1=0.647，复杂度为细粒度的65%）；粗粒度图在图级布局质量评估最有效（Spearman ρ=0.610，复杂度为细粒度的16%）。

Conclusion: 该研究实现了从平面图图像可扩展、免标注提取结构化布局信息，推动设计信息学发展，将平面图转化为知识表示，增强设计信息在建筑全生命周期中的利用。

Abstract: Architectural floorplan images encode rich relational knowledge among functional spaces, which underpins design retrieval, knowledge-based reasoning, and BIM enrichment through the building lifecycle. However, it remains challenging to automatically construct task-adaptive graph representations for public buildings. To address this gap, we first define a multi-granularity Level-of-Graphs (LoGs) for public building layouts. Methodologically, we present a Vision-Language Model (VLM)-based automatic LoG construction through node identification, edge inference, text parsing, and graph coarsening. VLM-generated representations are systematically evaluated and tested in real-world tasks, using 147 academic library floorplans worldwide as a case study. Experiments showed VLM-generated graphs were broadly consistent with human-labeled graphs (matched node ratio >= 92%; 509.3 s per floor plan for three-LoG graph generation). Meso-grained graphs yield the best node-level zone prediction (Macro F1 = 0.647, at 65% of fine-grained complexity), while coarse-grained graphs are most effective for graph-level layout quality evaluation (Spearman's \r{ho} = 0.610, at 16% of fine-grained complexity). By enabling scalable, annotation-free extraction of structured layout information from floorplan images, this study advances design informatics by converting plan images into knowledge representations, thereby enhancing the utilization of design information across the building life cycle.

</details>


### [193] [SIMGUIDE: Procedurally Grounded Multi-Context Representations for Personalized Agent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24888)
*Chirag Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SIMGUIDE 方法，将用户上下文结构化为带有程序化示例的类型化、领域特定块（Sims），并构建 SIMBENCH 基准测试。实验表明：程序化接地的 Sims 显著优于 RAG 个性化（GPT-4o 上 +7.9 Preference Adherence，p=0.013），在 τ-bench 任务上跨模型复现；参数层面，任务匹配的 LoRA 微调提升 12.8 ROUGE-L，按 Sim 类型路由适配器再增 7.3 点，且对 28% 路由错误鲁棒。核心结论：表征格式而非内容是个性化的一阶设计变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化智能体将用户视为扁平画像（拼接进提示词），无法处理同一用户在不同生活语境下的优先级差异及冲突。问题不在于缺乏用户信息，而在于用户表征的格式决定了智能体能否利用这些信息。

Method: 1. SIMGUIDE：将用户上下文结构化为类型化、领域特定的 Sims 块，并用过往决策的程序化示例接地每个约束。2. SIMBENCH：包含 47 个偏好条件规划任务的诊断套件，正确计划取决于激活的用户语境。3. 参数层实验：任务匹配的 LoRA 微调，以及按 Sim 类型而非用户身份路由适配器。

Result: 声明式 Sims 约束不优于 RAG；程序化接地的 Sims 在 GPT-4o 上比 RAG 高出 7.9 Preference Adherence 点（p=0.013），在 100 个 τ-bench 任务上跨 GPT-4o 和 Claude Sonnet 4.5 复现（p≤0.023）。LoRA 微调比未适配基座模型提升 12.8 ROUGE-L，按 Sim 类型路由再增 7.3 点，对 28% 路由错误鲁棒。

Conclusion: 表征格式——而非表征内容——是个性化系统的一阶设计变量。将上下文结构化为类型化、程序化接地的块，使智能体能跨语境利用用户信息；参数适配亦遵循同一原则：训练分布匹配任务结构才能生效。

Abstract: Personalized AI agents overwhelmingly treat users as single entities: a flat profile concatenated into a prompt. This fails when the same person holds different priorities across life contexts -- and fails catastrophically when those priorities conflict. The core problem is not that agents lack information about users; it is that the format of user representations determines whether an agent can act on that information at all. We introduce SIMGUIDE, a method that structures user context into typed, domain-specific blocks called Sims and grounds each constraint with procedural examples drawn from past decisions. To evaluate this, we construct SIMBENCH, a diagnostic suite of 47 preference-conditioned planning tasks where the correct plan depends on which user context is active -- a property no existing benchmark tests. Declarative Sim constraints alone do not outperform retrieval-based personalization (RAG). Procedurally grounded Sims outperform RAG on GPT-4o (+7.9 Preference Adherence points, $p = 0.013$), and this advantage replicates on 100 $τ$-bench tasks across both GPT-4o and Claude Sonnet~4.5 ($p \leq 0.023$). At the parametric level, the same principle holds: training distribution dominates whether parametric adaptation succeeds at all. Task-matched LoRA fine-tuning improves generation quality by 12.8 ROUGE-L points over the unadapted base model, and routing adapters by Sim type rather than user identity adds a further 7.3 points, robust to 28% routing error. Representation format -- not representation content -- is the first-order design variable.

</details>


### [194] [Reliable LLM-Powered Decision Engines for Large-Scale Supply Chain Operations: Architecture, Safety, and Performance Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24889)
*Nirmal Kumar Jingar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种名为 LLM-Powered Decision Engine (LLM-DE) 的混合架构，将大语言模型与数学优化、概率预测和安全约束决策过滤相结合，用于大规模供应链的端到端决策（需求预测、库存优化、运输路由、干扰缓解），实现了语义推理与形式化安全保证的融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模供用链面临高不确定性、强动态性和频繁干扰，传统基于规则和纯优化的系统难以提供及时且具韧性的决策；异构数据源（交易需求信号、非结构化干扰报告）的增多为智能系统提供了机会，需兼具推理、适应与优化能力。

Method: 构建混合架构 LLM-DE：利用 LLM 进行语义推理与非结构化数据理解，结合数学优化模型（库存、路由等）、概率预测模块（需求预测）以及安全约束决策过滤层，形成完整的算法管线与数学化运营决策系统。

Result: 实验表明，融合语言推理与优化及形式约束的方法能生成更智能、更安全、可扩展的供应链决策，在需求预测、库存优化、运输路由及干扰缓解等端到端任务上优于纯数据驱动或启发式方案。

Conclusion: LLM-DE 为下一代智能供应链基础设施提供了务实且有理论支撑的架构，能在不确定性和大规模复杂性下可靠运行，推动了 LLM 与运筹优化的深度融合应用。

Abstract: Current large-scale supply chains are highly uncertain, dynamic, and disruption prone that are challenging to serve up timely and resilient decisions through traditional rule-based and optimization-only systems. The increasing supply of heterogeneous data sources, such as transactional demand signals and unstructured disruption report, presents a chance of intelligent systems, which could reason, adapt and optimize at the same time. A hybrid architecture that combines large language models (LLMs) with mathematical optimization, probabilistic forecasting, and safety-constrained decision filtering is proposed in this paper as a performance of a Decision Engine, which is called LLM-Powered Decision Engine (LLM-DE). In comparison to purely data-driven or heuristic solutions, LLM-DE integrates semantic reasoning with LLM with a set of performance and safety guarantees that allow safe decision-making in large-scale supply chain processes. The suggested framework enables the end-to-end decision making such as demand forecasting, inventory optimization, and transportation routing and disruption mitigation. The findings affirm that language-based reasoning combined with optimization and formal constraints can be used to come up with not only smarter but also safer and more scalable supply chain decisions. This research provides a new architecture, a complete pipeline of algorithm, and a formulation based on mathematical constructs of the operational decision systems incorporating LLM. The proposed model offers a pragmatic and theoretical basis of the next-generation intelligent supply chain infrastructures that can be implemented to work dependably in the face of uncertainty and massive complexity.

</details>


### [195] [AI-Powered Mental Health Chatbots in Africa: A Systematic Review and Culturally Adaptive Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24890)
*Matshepo Lebese,Pitso Tsibolane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 系统回顾52项实证研究，发现AI聊天机器人在非洲心理健康领域具潜力，但严重缺乏本地化文化适配，提出文化适应性数字心理健康（CADMH）框架以整合非洲文化价值观、多语言设计、移动优先优化及伦理保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲心理健康挑战因基础设施匮乏、污名化及专业人员长期短缺而长期未获充分解决，AI驱动聊天机器人作为低成本、高可及性的心理支持工具在全球兴起，但其在非洲语境下的文化、语言及基础设施适用性尚未系统评估。

Method: 对2017-2025年间发表的52项实证研究进行系统回顾，批判性分析其与非洲语境的文化、语言及基础设施相关性。

Result: 证实AI聊天机器人可提升可及性、缓解焦虑抑郁症状、扩大心理社会支持，但多数系统仍植根于西方模型与英语设计，存在本地相关性、包容性及可持续性的关键缺口；据此构建了文化适应性数字心理健康（CADMH）框架，整合非洲文化价值观、多语言设计、移动优先优化及伦理保障。

Conclusion: 研究揭示将AI聊天机器人融入非洲医疗体系的机遇与障碍，为研究、实践及政策制定提供指导，呼吁开发文化敏感、语言包容、技术可及的数字心理健康解决方案以实现可持续发展。

Abstract: Mental health challenges in Africa remain under-addressed due to inadequate infrastructure, stigma, and a chronic shortage of professionals. Artificial Intelligence (AI)-powered chatbots are emerging globally as low-cost, accessible tools that can offer psychological support. This paper presents a systematic review of 52 empirical studies published between 2017 and 2025, critically analysing their cultural, linguistic, and infrastructural relevance to African contexts. The findings demonstrate the potential of AI chatbots to improve accessibility, reduce symptoms of anxiety and depression, and expand psychosocial support, yet reveal limited African-specific adaptation. Most systems remain rooted in Western models and English-language designs, leaving critical gaps in local relevance, inclusivity, and sustainability. Based on the findings, the authors develop a Culturally Adaptive Digital Mental Health (CADMH) framework that integrates African cultural values, multilingual design, mobile-first optimisation, and ethical safeguards. The study highlights opportunities and barriers for integrating AI chatbots into African healthcare, offering guidance for research, practice, and policy.

</details>


### [196] [Measurement-Budget Allocation in Quantum Learning with Finite-Shot Generalization Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24891)
*Ferhat Ozgur Catak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究近期量子硬件上二分类任务的测量预算分配问题，证明了一个与分布无关的泛化界，分离了有限样本和有限测量的贡献，并给出了最优分配规则 n* 和 S*，实验验证了理论界的保守性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在近期量子硬件上，估计 Born 概率需要重复电路执行。给定固定的总测量预算 B，必须决定使用多少不同训练态 n 以及每个态分配多少测量次数 S。这种权衡直接影响量子分类器的泛化性能，但此前缺乏理论指导。

Method: 针对固定或独立选择的测量算子 M 的二分类量子分类器，证明了一个分布无关的泛化界。该界包含两项：样本项随 sqrt(d/n) 缩放，测量项随 sqrt((log n)/S) 缩放。在约束 B = nS 下，最小化界的保守闭式替代式，得到分配规则 n* = 2√(2dB / log(2B/δ))，S* = B/n*。并通过 PennyLane 模拟 2-qubit 和 4-qubit 变分量子电路在九个合成二分类基准上的表现进行验证。

Result: 理论最优分配规则的渐近缩放为 B^{-1/4}。在所有测试配置中，单侧经验泛化间隙均低于理论界，验证了界的保守性。结果为有限测量评估和预实验规划提供了保守的统计指导。

Conclusion: 本工作为近期量子学习系统中的测量预算分配提供了理论基础，补充了硬件调度和电路设计考量。将保证扩展到完全自适应的含噪训练仍是开放问题。

Abstract: On near-term quantum hardware, estimating a Born probability requires repeated circuit executions. A quantum learning experiment with a fixed measurement budget $B$ must therefore decide how many distinct training states $n$ to use and how many shots $S$ to allocate to each state. We study this tradeoff for binary quantum classifiers with fixed or independently selected measurement operators $M$, where the ideal score is $\Tr(Mρ)$. We prove a distribution-free generalization bound that separates the finite-sample and finite-shot contributions. The sample term scales as $\sqrt{d/n}$, while the shot term scales as $\sqrt{(\log n)/S}$; under the constraint $B=nS$, these two terms move in opposite directions. Minimising a conservative closed-form surrogate of the bound gives the allocation rule $\nstar = 2\sqrt{2dB/\log(2B/δ)}$ and $\Sstar = B/\nstar$. This surrogate has the same asymptotic scaling as the exact minimizer and yields a worst-case rate of $B^{-1/4}$. The guarantee is intentionally conservative, since it applies to the full class of binary quantum measurements. We complement the theory with PennyLane simulations using 2-qubit and 4-qubit variational quantum circuits on nine synthetic binary classification benchmarks. In all tested configurations, the one-sided empirical generalization gap remains below the theoretical bound. The result provides a conservative statistical guideline for allocating measurement budgets in finite-shot evaluation and pre-experimental planning for near-term quantum learning systems, complementing hardware-level scheduling and circuit-design considerations. Extending the guarantee to fully adaptive shot-noisy training remains an open problem.

</details>


### [197] [Natural Language Input, Semantic Track Representation, and LLM Inference: Making the Maritime Information Exchange Model Tractable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24892)
*Frederick Roth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用大语言模型（LLM）实现海洋信息交换模型（MIEM）和富语义轨迹模型的实用架构，通过自然语言输入自动生成类型化语义断言记录（SAR），构建知识图谱，并进行推理、异常检测和假设排序，使语义轨迹模型在国防和执法领域即刻可部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决语义轨迹模型在国防和执法领域采用受阻的问题，即操作员需学习形式本体语言并手动将观察编码为类型化逻辑断言的障碍。

Method: 架构包含四步：1) 操作员以自然语言贡献观察；2) LLM将自然语言翻译为类型化语义断言记录（SAR），即捕获 n 元关系的命名案例框架；3) 知识图谱累积 SAR；4) 第二轮 LLM 过图谱执行推理、异常检测和假设排序。通过 9/11 赛前指标场景和海洋货物检查场景两个详细示例演示全流程。

Result: 展示了从自然语言输入到 SAR 表示再到推理输出的完整管道，验证了架构的可行性；论证该架构使轨迹模型和 MIEM 能立即用现有技术部署，确立了防止专有封闭的现有技术，并将方法奠基于连接语义轨迹表示与神经流形几何的理论框架。

Conclusion: 该架构消除了操作员学习本体语言的门槛，利用当前 LLM 技术使语义轨迹模型即刻可部署，提供了理论依据和先例，推动了国防和执法领域的语义信息交换应用。

Abstract: We describe a practical architecture for making the Maritime Information Exchange Model (MIEM) and the broader Rich Semantic Track model tractable using current large language model (LLM) technology. The barrier to adoption of semantic track models in defense and law enforcement has been the requirement that operators learn formal ontology languages and manually encode observations as typed logical assertions. We propose eliminating this barrier entirely: operators contribute observations in natural language; an LLM translates these into typed Semantic Assertion Records (SARs), which are named case frames that capture n-ary relations in a single compact structure; a knowledge graph accumulates the SARs; and a second LLM pass performs inference, anomaly detection, and hypothesis ranking over the graph. We work through two detailed examples (a 9/11-era pre-attack indicator scenario and a maritime cargo inspection scenario) showing the full pipeline from natural language input to SAR representation to inference output. We argue that this architecture makes the Track Model and MIEM immediately deployable with current technology, establishes prior art against proprietary enclosure of the approach, and grounds the method in a theoretical framework connecting semantic track representations to neural manifold geometry.

</details>


### [198] [Same-Player Verification for Account Consistency in Counter-Strike 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24893)
*Xuchen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于CS2演示回放的同一玩家验证方法，通过提取行为指纹（准星控制、移动停顿开火配合、经济购买、战斗交战、时间节奏）并训练成对模型，实现了账号历史行为一致性检测，单对AUC达0.931，聚合10场历史后AUC提升至0.986。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在竞技性FPS游戏（如CS2）中，账号完整性审核需要判断近期行为是否与历史操作者一致，涉及临时替补、代练、高手用小号等场景，人工审核需对比多场历史比赛，亟需自动化的同一玩家验证技术。

Method: 从1330个CS2演示文件中提取13300个演示-玩家观测，构建涵盖准星控制、移动停顿开火配合、经济购买、战斗交战、时间节奏的行为指纹；从8840万候选对中采样663590个同一/不同玩家对进行监督训练；训练成对模型判断两个行为观测是否来自同一真实玩家；并评估账号历史聚合（K=1到K=10）对验证性能的影响。

Result: 最终成对模型平均ROC AUC达0.931，在95%精度下不同玩家召回率为0.722；特征分析显示底层操作（准星控制、开火节奏、移动停顿开火配合）提供最强身份信号；账号历史聚合评估中，AUC从K=1的0.931提升至K=10的0.986。

Conclusion: CS2演示行为可支持有监督的同一玩家验证，通过多演示历史聚合可实现账号级身份一致性建模，底层机械操作习惯比单场比赛表现结果更具验证价值。

Abstract: In competitive first-person shooter (FPS) games such as Counter-Strike 2 (CS2), account-integrity review often asks whether an account's recent behavior remains consistent with its historical operator. This consistency question arises in cases such as temporary substitution, rank boosting, and high-skill players using lower-ranked accounts, where manual review requires comparing a current match against multiple historical matches. We formulate this review task as same-player verification: we encode the behavioral trajectory of a single player in a match replay (demo) as a demo-player behavioral fingerprint, and train a model to judge whether two behavioral observations come from the same real player. Grounded in CS2 game understanding, the fingerprints cover crosshair control, movement-stop-fire coordination, economy/buy, combat/engagement, and temporal rhythm. From 1,330 CS2 demos we extract 13,300 demo-player observations, and sample 663,590 same/different pairs from an 88.4M candidate-pair space for supervised training and evaluation. The final pairwise model reaches an average ROC AUC of 0.931 and achieves 0.722 different-player recall at 95% precision. Feature analysis shows that the strongest identity signals come from low-level operations, especially crosshair control, firing rhythm, and movement-stop-fire coordination, indicating that stable low-level mechanical habits are more informative for this verification task than single-match performance outcomes. In the account-history aggregation evaluation, increasing history depth raises AUC from the K=1 single-pair baseline of 0.931 to 0.986 at K=10. These results show that CS2 demo behavior can support supervised same-player verification and account-level identity-consistency modeling through multi-demo history aggregation.

</details>


### [199] [Semantic Graph Unification for Industrial Digital Threads: Bridging 11 Heterogeneous Manufacturing Systems Through Ontology-Driven Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24918)
*Grama Chethan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于本体驱动的RDF知识图谱框架，用于统一异构工业系统（ERP、MES、PLM等）的数字线索，通过五阶段ETL管道和自动实体解析整合11个模拟数据源，并利用九类发现策略跨系统挖掘洞察，实验表明69%的可发现信号依赖跨系统图连接，且在五个行业模板中展示了模式稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代制造企业运行着多个异构系统（ERP、MES、PLM、SCADA、QMS、SCM），各自拥有独立数据模型和API，形成数据孤岛，阻碍整体分析、根因调查和工业4.0追溯性；点对点集成规模呈O(n^2)且依赖脆弱，亟需统一语义框架。

Method: 构建基于本体的RDF知识图谱，涵盖78个RDFS类、108个对象属性、243个数据属性，引用ISA-95、OPC UA、eClass、资产管理外壳、RAMI 4.0等标准；设计五阶段ETL管道，自动实体解析建立97个owl:sameAs同一性链接，整合11个模拟数据源跨九个领域；开发自动发现引擎，应用九大策略类别（跨工位相关、告警覆盖、ECN影响、CUSUM/EWMA漂移检测等）跨系统边界挖掘洞察；通过287个Model Context Protocol工具向LLM代理暴露SPARQL原生语义层；提供五个行业模板验证模式稳定性。

Result: 主要实证结果：阻断24个跨系统工具导致召回率从1.00降至0.31（F1从1.00降至0.48），表明69%的可发现信号需要跨系统图连接；留一法消融实验确认九大策略中有六种贡献独特信号；针对65信号清单（16阳性、49阴性）验证得F1=1.00（95% Clopper-Pearson CI [0.79, 1.00]），但清单为作者自建，属验证而非独立验证。

Conclusion: 该开放框架通过本体驱动的语义图统一工业数字线索，有效打破数据孤岛，实现跨系统洞察发现，并在多个制造垂直领域展示了模式稳定性，为工业4.0追溯性和整体分析提供了可扩展的语义层基础设施。

Abstract: Modern manufacturing enterprises operate heterogeneous systems -- ERP, MES, PLM, SCADA, QMS, SCM -- each with its own data model and API. The resulting silos prevent holistic analysis, delay root-cause investigation, and obstruct Industry 4.0 traceability. Point-to-point integration scales as O(n^2) and accumulates brittle dependencies. This paper presents an open framework for semantic graph unification of industrial digital threads. An ontology-driven RDF knowledge graph unifies data from 11 simulated sources across nine domains through a five-stage ETL pipeline with automated entity resolution spanning 97 owl:sameAs identity links. The ontology encompasses 78 RDFS classes, 108 object properties, and 243 data properties, drawing on ISA-95, OPC UA, eClass, the Asset Administration Shell, RAMI 4.0, and additional standards. An automated discovery engine applies nine strategy categories -- cross-station correlation, alarm coverage, ECN impact, CUSUM/EWMA drift detection -- to surface insights spanning system boundaries. The primary empirical result: blocking 24 cross-system tools reduces recall from 1.00 to 0.31 (F1 from 1.00 to 0.48), showing that 69% of discoverable signals require cross-system graph joins. Leave-one-out ablation confirms six of nine strategies contribute unique signals. Verification against a 65-signal manifest (16 positive, 49 null) yields F1 = 1.00 (95% Clopper-Pearson CI [0.79, 1.00]); as the manifest was author-constructed, this constitutes verification not independent validation. The graph is exposed to LLM agents via 287 Model Context Protocol tools as a SPARQL-native semantic layer. Five industry templates (aerospace, CPG, pharma, medical devices, turbine blades) demonstrate schema stability across manufacturing verticals.

</details>


### [200] [post-graph-rag: A PostgreSQL-Native Graph RAG Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24921)
*Chandan Rajah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: post-graph-rag 是一个开源引擎，通过单一 PostgreSQL 数据库整合向量检索、实体图与社区摘要，并在抽取时施加严格不变量与时间层，解决了图增强检索生成（Graph-RAG）在基础设施复杂度、图谱质量与时间演化三大痛点。实验显示其构建的图更稠密、边标签更规范、查询延迟更低，并能正确处理事实被取代与时间点查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Graph-RAG 实现需同时维护向量库、图数据库与文档库（基础设施成本高）；抽取管线从不拒绝输出，导致图中充斥模糊谓词、代词指代与裸露数量等低质量边（图谱质量差）；图谱仅单向累积，无法区分被取代事实与当前事实，也不支持时间点查询（时间演化缺失）。

Method: 1. 统一存储：文本块向量、规范实体图、社区摘要全部存入单一 PostgreSQL，配合 pgvector 向量检索与边表图遍历。2. 抽取时不变量：拒绝模糊谓词、代词实体名、裸露数量；谓词可映射至受控词表；实体经模型提供的别名归一化为唯一顶点；否定关系保留正向谓词并打标记。3. 时间层：关系携带文本声明的有效期；后到文档自动覆盖先前冲突断言；支持 as-of 时间点查询。

Result: 在三个语料上对比 LightRAG（同抽取与嵌入模型）：post-graph-rag 实体平均关系数最高提升 2.4×；不同边标签占比 0.46–0.58（受控词表下 0.11），显著低于基线 0.77–1.33；查询延迟更低且答案质量相当；在小说序列与十年公文语料中分别检测到 13 与 8 组被取代关系，基线为零。

Conclusion: post-graph-rag 以工程指标而非基准测试形式证明：单库架构+抽取约束+时间层可同时降低基础设施复杂度、提升图谱质量并原生支持事实演化与时间查询。代码已开源（github.com/crajah/post-graph-rag、github.com/crajah/post-graph）。

Abstract: Graph-based retrieval-augmented generation connects facts that no single passage states, but current implementations pay for that three times: in infrastructure, requiring a vector store, graph database and document store to be kept consistent; in graph quality, because an extraction pipeline that never refuses output fills the graph with edges that assert nothing; and over time, because a graph that only accumulates treats superseded and current facts alike.
  post-graph-rag is an open-source engine addressing all three. Text chunks with embeddings, a canonical entity graph and community summaries live in one PostgreSQL database, with pgvector for search and edge tables for traversal. Extraction-time invariants run before anything is written: vague predicates, pronominal names and bare quantities are rejected; predicates are normalised onto an optional vocabulary; entities resolve to one vertex per canonical name via model-supplied aliases; and denied relations keep the positive predicate under a negation flag. A temporal layer lets relations carry a validity period from the prose, lets a later document supersede an earlier incompatible assertion from document order alone, and answers as-of queries.
  Against LightRAG on three corpora under identical extraction and embedding models, post-graph-rag builds a denser graph everywhere, up to $2.4\times$ the relations per entity, and a more queryable one: distinct edge labels run at 0.46 to 0.58 per relation, 0.11 under a controlled vocabulary, against 0.77 to 1.33. It answers comparably with lower query latency, and supports temporal evolution the baseline lacks: 13 and 8 relationships superseded on a novel sequence and a decade of filings, against zero. These are engineering measurements, not a benchmark result.
  Code: https://github.com/crajah/post-graph-rag, https://github.com/crajah/post-graph

</details>


### [201] [FrontierChallenge: Evaluating Scientific Workflow Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24979)
*Liangcai Su,Zhaopeng Feng,Zhuo Chen,Zhen Zhang,Xiang Lin,Ruilin Li,Handuo Zhang,Ning Wang,Kailong Wen,Yueqi Guo,Feng Xing,Yiling Guo,Chenxiong Qian,Simon Shaolei Du,Lidong Bing,Xinyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了 FrontierChallenge，一个包含 300 个端到端科学工作流的跨领域基准，发布了其中 97 个任务，覆盖量子化学、分子动力学、材料表征、分析化学、生命科学和电化学/环境六大领域。评估了 12 个前沿模型在三种代理脚手架下的表现，使用 Pass Rate（完全完成率）和 Avg. Score（平均部分得分）两个指标。最佳配置仅完成 20/97 任务，Pass Rate 20.6%。部分进展在分析化学和电化学/环境领域难以转化为完整交付：Avg. Score 分别高达 87.6 和 94.9，但最高 Pass Rate 仅为 4% 和 0%。未通过的 Claude Code 轨迹中 75.5% 仍声称完成。研究表明，高部分得分或自信的完成声称不能可靠地指示科学任务已完全交付，强调需同时评估端到端工作流执行和科学交付物的完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多关注最终答案、孤立程序或单一领域，缺乏对跨领域端到端科学工作流的全面评估，无法反映科学代理在真实科研中完成完整交付物的能力。

Method: 构建 FrontierChallenge 基准，包含 300 个端到端科学工作流，发布 97 个任务，每个任务提供固定输入并指定一组必需的科学交付物。评估 12 个前沿模型在三种代理脚手架下的表现，采用 Pass Rate（满足完全完成标准的任务比例）和 Avg. Score（捕捉部分进展）两个指标。

Result: 最佳配置仅完成 20/97 任务，Pass Rate 20.6%。分析化学和电化学/环境领域 Avg. Score 分别达 87.6 和 94.9，但最高 Pass Rate 仅 4% 和 0%。未通过的 Claude Code 轨迹中 75.5% 仍声称完成。部分进展与完整交付之间存在显著鸿沟，且模型常错误地声称任务已完成。

Conclusion: 高部分得分或自信的完成声称不能可靠指示科学任务已完全交付。未来评估需结合端到端工作流执行能力和科学交付物的完整性，以更准确衡量科学代理的实际效用。

Abstract: Scientific agents increasingly analyze data, execute code, and produce research artifacts, yet most benchmarks emphasize final answers, isolated programs, or a single domain. We introduce FrontierChallenge, a cross-domain benchmark comprising 300 end-to-end scientific workflows. In this paper, we release and evaluate 97 of these tasks, spanning quantum chemistry, molecular dynamics, materials characterization, analytical chemistry, life science, and electrochemistry/environment. Each task provides fixed inputs and specifies a bundle of required scientific deliverables. We evaluate twelve frontier models with three agent scaffolds. Pass Rate measures the fraction of tasks satisfying the full-completion criterion, while Avg. Score captures partial progress. Each of the best-performing configurations completed only 20 of the 97 released tasks, yielding a Pass Rate of 20.6%. Partial progress translated especially poorly into complete delivery in analytical chemistry and electrochemistry/environment: Avg. Scores reached 87.6 and 94.9, but the highest Pass Rates were only 4% and 0%. Among non-passing Claude Code trajectories, 75.5% still ended with language claiming completion. These findings show that neither high partial scores nor confident claims of completion reliably indicate that a scientific task has been fully delivered, highlighting the need to evaluate end-to-end workflow execution and the completeness of scientific deliverables together.

</details>


### [202] [Solving Robust POMDPs with Omega-regular Objectives via Partially Observable Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24986)
*Durgam Latha,Dion Reji,S. Akshay,Djordje Zikelic,Shankaranarayanan Krishna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明了带有多面体不确定集的(s,a)-矩形鲁棒部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程(RPOMDP)在omega-正则目标下求解的问题，可以归约为部分可观测随机博弈(POSG)，并建立了二者的语义等价性，从而推导出一系列新的计算复杂度上下界结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典POMDP假设转移概率精确已知，但在实际应用中往往只知道概率属于某个不确定集。RPOMDP将POMDP推广到此设定。本文旨在研究RPOMDP在广泛的omega-正则目标（包括到达性、安全性、LTL等）下的求解问题，填补该领域的理论空白。

Method: 针对带有多面体不确定集的(s,a)-矩形RPOMDP，构造了到POSG在omega-正则目标下的归约，并首次证明了双向归约的存在，确立了两者的语义等价性。基于这种等价性，利用POSG已有的复杂度理论，推导RPOMDP在不同omega-正则目标下的计算复杂度上界和下界。

Result: 获得了RPOMDP在多种omega-正则目标下求解的新计算复杂度结果（包括上界和下界）；作为推论，也为鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程(RMDP)得到了新的复杂度结果。

Conclusion: RPOMDP与POSG的语义等价性为鲁棒规划提供了统一理论框架，使得可以在两个模型间迁移算法和复杂度结果，显著推进了在不确定环境下omega-正则目标鲁棒规划的理论理解。

Abstract: Robust POMDPs (RPOMDPs) generalize classical POMDPs to the setting where exact transition probabilities are not known -- rather, they are only known to belong to some uncertainty set of values. In this work, we study the problem of solving RPOMDPs with general omega-regular objectives, which subsume a broad class of objectives such as reachability, safety, and linear temporal logic (LTL) objectives. We show that, for (s,a)-rectangular RPOMDPs with polytopic uncertainty sets, the problem of solving RPOMDPs under omega-regular objectives can be reduced to solving partially observable stochastic games (POSGs) under omega-regular objectives. Moreover, we show for the first time that reductions can be constructed in both directions, establishing the semantic equivalence between (s,a)-rectangular RPOMDPs with polytopic uncertainty sets and POSGs. This allows us to derive a range of new computational complexity results, including both upper and lower complexity bounds, on solving RPOMDPs with different omega-regular objectives. As a corollary, we also derive new computational complexity results for RMDPs.

</details>


### [203] [CVE-SAI: Counterfactual Visual Evidence-Guided Selective Attribute Indexing for Risk-Controlled E-commerce Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25023)
*Xiaolong Sun,Qichao Wang,Hangyu Li,Liang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CVE-SAI的反事实视觉证据引导选择性属性索引方法，解决了多模态商品模型在电商属性补全中忽视视觉支持验证、混淆瞬时预测与持久索引准入、缺乏错误值风险控制的问题。通过将推理与准入分离，实现了视觉支持的属性补全，提升检索性能同时限制索引污染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态商品模型在电商属性补全中存在三大缺陷：1) 仅优化属性-答案准确性而不验证视觉支持；2) 混淆瞬时预测与持久索引准入；3) 缺乏对事实上错误或视觉不支持值的显式风险控制。这些缺陷导致索引污染和检索性能下降。

Method: 提出CVE-SAI框架，包含两个核心组件：1) Focus-Zone Distortion (FZD)：通过受控反事实干预构建属性特定的视觉依赖代理；2) Evidence-Guided Attention Redistribution (EGAR)：利用代理细化本体约束评分。流程：先从主图和属性问题推理并冻结本体约束候选（不使用目录文本），再决定候选是否入索。候选冻结后需通过证据必要性、证据保留、噪声变换稳定性、候选特定目录文本冲突审计；目录文本仅能收紧准入不能修改候选。采用独立家族层面校准，在5%不安全准入预算下选择单一策略。

Result: 在Amazon Berkeley Objects数据集的五个视觉属性上实验表明：CVE-SAI改进了属性推理和证据定位；在共享风险协议下实现最高认证准入覆盖率；在自动准入系统中获得最强受控检索性能和最低不安全自动诱导暴露。

Conclusion: 将推理与准入分离，使得视觉支持的属性补全能够提升检索性能同时限制持久索引污染，为电商属性补全提供了更安全可靠的范式。

Abstract: Multimodal product models can complete missing e-commerce attributes, yet current methods still optimize attribute-answer accuracy without verifying visual support, conflate transient prediction with persistent index admission, and lack explicit risk control over factually incorrect or visually unsupported values. We address these gaps with Counterfactual Visual Evidence-Guided Selective Attribute Indexing (CVE-SAI), which first infers and freezes an ontology-constrained candidate from the primary image and attribute question without catalog text, and then decides whether that candidate should enter the index. Focus-Zone Distortion (FZD) constructs an attribute-specific visual-dependence proxy through a controlled counterfactual intervention, and Evidence-Guided Attention Redistribution (EGAR) uses the proxy to refine ontology-constrained scoring. The canonical candidate is frozen before evidence necessity, evidence retention, nuisance-transformation stability, and candidate-specific catalog-text conflict audits; catalog text can only tighten admission and cannot revise the candidate. Independent family-level calibration selects one policy with a simultaneous one-sided finite-sample bound under a 5% unsafe-admission budget. Experiments on five visual attributes derived from Amazon Berkeley Objects show that CVE-SAI improves attribute inference and evidence localization, achieves the highest certified admission coverage under the shared risk protocol, and yields the strongest controlled retrieval performance with the lowest unsafe auto-induced exposure among automatic-admission systems. Separating inference from admission therefore enables visually supported attribute completion to improve retrieval while limiting persistent index contamination.

</details>


### [204] [Retrieve, Match, Escalate: Accurate and Scalable Product Linking with VLM-Distilled Cross-Encoders and Agentic VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25037)
*Jian Wang,Steven Xu,Sanjyot Thete,Maryam Barouti,Tom Tang,Elaine Wu,Charu Sareen,Kyle MacDonald*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种用于大规模商品实体链接的生产级检索-匹配级联系统：先用轻量级跨编码器自动处理高置信度样本，再用自托管的开源多模态视觉-语言智能体处理难例，实现了在极低成本下将端到端链接覆盖率从 68% 提升至 77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 市场级商品链接面临海量噪声记录与海量规范商品的匹配难题：单一模型要么在难例上表现不足，要么在易例上成本过高，需要一种按难度分配计算资源的级联方案。

Method: 1. 检索阶段召回候选；2. 轻量级文本跨编码器（从数百万双 VLM 共识标签蒸馏，无需人工标注）校准至 98% 精度自动通过高置信链接；3. 对模棱两可的剩余样本，调用自托管开源多模态 VLM 智能体，结合商品图像与网络搜索证据进行裁决，无需微调即可达到闭源前沿 VLM 的精度。

Result: 跨编码器阶段覆盖 68% 链接，级联后整体覆盖率达 77%。智能体以约 1/7 的单对成本达到前沿 VLM 精度，召回仅低 4 个百分点（88% 对 92%）。单对成本跨度近 5 个数量级，仅将难例上传智能体即可大幅提升覆盖率。

Conclusion: 检索-匹配级联结合蒸馏跨编码器与开源多模态智能体，能在极低边际成本下显著提升大规模商品链接的覆盖率与精度，展示了按难度动态分配计算资源的实用范式。

Abstract: Product linking, the entity-resolution task of mapping merchant product records to canonical catalog products, consolidates fragmented listings so downstream search, recommendation, and advertising see one clean entry per product. At marketplace scale, billions of noisy, multi-category records must be resolved against tens of millions of canonical products, where scoring every candidate with a single model is either too weak for the hard cases or too costly for the easy ones. We present a production retrieve-then-match cascade that spends computation in proportion to difficulty: retrieval surfaces plausible matches, a lightweight text cross-encoder auto-resolves the high-confidence majority, and an agentic multimodal vision-language model settles the ambiguous remainder by inspecting product images and issuing web searches for evidence that is in neither record. The cross-encoder is distilled from millions of dual-VLM-consensus labels, retiring human annotation from the training set, and is calibrated to auto-accept links at a 98% precision bar validated against a smaller operator-certified audit. The agent is a self-hosted open-weight model that reaches a closed frontier VLM's precision at a four-point recall cost (88% versus 92%) for roughly one-seventh the per-pair cost, with no fine-tuning. Per-pair cost spans nearly five orders of magnitude from the cheap cross-encoder to the frontier VLM, so escalating only the hard tail to the agent raises end-to-end link coverage from the cheap stage's 68% to 77%.

</details>


### [205] [LifePlanner: Evaluating LLM Agents for Geo-spatial Planning with Social Media Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25039)
*Zhen Dong,Yuning Peng,Yutao Shi,Lei Zhong,Yongsen Mao,Yuan Liu,Haiping Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LifePlanner 是一个地理空间规划基准，融合大规模社交媒体数据与地图数据，通过 MCP 工具集评估 LLM 代理在多约束推理、工具使用和证据检索上的表现。实验显示前沿 LLM 在简单检索任务表现良好，但在复杂规划任务通过率降至 40.2%，失败主要源于证据获取不全、工具使用不精准、约束整合薄弱，而非模型规模或推理长度，提示未来需聚焦有效落地规划而非单纯扩大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理空间规划基准多提供干净的地理空间数据和工具，缺乏人们日常规划中使用的开放式社交信号，无法全面评估 LLM 代理在真实噪声环境下的工具使用、证据检索和多约束推理能力。

Method: 构建 LifePlanner 基准，将大规模本地社交媒体帖子与地图数据融合，并通过 MCP 工具集提供访问接口。设计涵盖四类任务、三个难度等级的评估套件，对前沿 LLM 进行系统实验。

Result: 前沿 LLM 在简单检索任务表现优异，但在复杂规划任务上通过率大幅下降至 40.2%。错误分析表明，失败主要归因于从大规模多模态数据库中证据获取不完整、工具使用不精准、约束整合能力薄弱，而非模型参数量或推理步数。

Conclusion: 单纯扩大模型规模不足以解决复杂地理空间规划问题，未来研究需聚焦于有效的落地规划能力，包括改进证据获取策略、工具使用精度和多约束整合机制。

Abstract: Geo-spatial planning, like trip design, is a realistic testbed for LLM agents because it requires grounded tool use, noisy evidence retrieval, and multi-constraint reasoning. Most benchmarks, however, only provide clean geospatial data and tools, missing the open-ended social signals that people use in daily planning. We introduce LifePlanner, a benchmark that enriches map data with large-scale local social media posts and provides access through an MCP toolset. LifePlanner provides an evaluation suite spanning four task categories and three difficulty levels. Experiments show frontier LLMs perform well on simple retrieval but degrade sharply on complex planning, with the Pass Rate dropping to 40.2%. Results show that failures mainly stem from incomplete evidence acquisition from such a large multimodal database, imprecise tool use, and weak constraint integration rather than model size or reasoning length, suggesting that future progress requires effective grounded planning instead of scaling alone.

</details>


### [206] [SimVerity: When Does Simulated Agent Success Survive Physical Deployment?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25067)
*Zhonghao Zhan,Yefan Zhang,Krinos Li,Hamed Haddadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SimVerity 框架，通过在真实智能家居部署中重放匹配场景并交叉验证物理见证者，量化模拟评估向物理部署的判决迁移可靠性。实验揭示模拟器全通过的 240 次灯光试验中，相机捕捉到 42 次亚秒级故障；基于实测试验学习的风险画像可在未实测路径上预测故障，且物理测量是唯一能暴露多模拟器共同盲区的手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟评估广泛用于 AI 智能体基准测试，但模拟通过能为物理部署提供多少证据尚未系统量化。需建立显式的判决迁移保障机制，在部署前决定“通过、弃权或升级”。

Method: SimVerity 框架：在目标智能家居部署上重放匹配场景，利用独立合格的物理见证者（如相机）交叉验证智能体执行；构建基于实测试验的风险画像预测未测路径故障；对比多模拟器与物理测量的一致性。

Result: 1) 同一执行中完成、上报状态、可观测效果、最终结果四维度出现分歧。2) 先进模拟器全通过 240 次试验，相机却捕获 42 次亚秒级故障。3) 风险画像在两组共 11 个保留会话中全部击败无属性基线。4) 更换智能体循环的模型客户端/服务配置使场景匹配率从 52-88% 提升至 100%。5) 第二合格模拟器在重叠案例上从未分歧，仅物理测量暴露共同盲区。

Conclusion: 部署成功是真实世界过程而非模拟中的静态属性。SimVerity 将判决迁移转化为显式决策（通过、弃权、升级），物理测量是揭示模拟器盲区的必要且不可替代的手段。

Abstract: Simulated evaluation is widely used to benchmark AI agents, yet how much evidence a simulated pass provides about physical deployment has not been systematically quantified. We present SimVerity, a verdict-transfer assurance framework: it replays matched scenarios on target smart home deployments and cross-validates agent execution against independently qualified physical witnesses. Our evaluation highlights that deployment success is a real-world process, not a static property in simulation: completion, reported state, observable effect, and settled outcome diverged within the same execution. Although an advanced simulator cleared all 240 light trials, a camera caught 42 sub-second failures invisible to settled-state checks. False clearance was predictable: a risk profile learned from measured trials and locked before evaluation predicted failures on a path it never physically measured, beating a property-blind baseline in all eleven held-out sessions across two cohorts. Agent auditability was also measurable: switching one agent loop's model-client/serving configuration raised its scenario-matching share from 52-88% to 100%. Finally, a second qualified simulator added no independent cross-check: it never disagreed on any overlapping case, and only physical measurement exposed their shared blind spots. SimVerity turns verdict transfer into an explicit decision: clear, abstain, or escalate before deployment.

</details>


### [207] [Auto-Policy, not Auto-Skill: Compiled Agent Skills for the Physical World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25091)
*Zhonghao Zhan,Hamed Haddadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出当前自进化技能（如AutoSkills、Hermes Agent）仅关注效率而非安全，提出了'借用权威'攻击类别：恶意技能可通过附加权限声明驱动物理执行。提出Edge Skillguard——嵌入技能制品内的类型化权威层，基于世界状态和传感器证据进行防护。在实时边缘控制平台测试中，该方案以零误拦截拒绝了全部60次跨5种变体的借用权威请求，且在5倍规模和Tailscale组网下保持有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前技能格式仅用Markdown和脚本描述'代理应如何行为'，而'哪些行为可被允许为动作'的策略完全交由模型决定。生成更多技能会扩大而非缩小这一安全鸿沟，特别是当错误调用可解锁门禁或转移资金时。已记录两类相邻攻击：恶意技能危害云软件、越狱LLM控制机器人造成物理伤害；其交集——恶意代理技能导致物理伤害——虽必然衍生但未被报道。核心缺陷在于技能格式未给接收代理提供类型化方式拒绝跨代理权限声明。

Method: 提出Edge Skillguard：一种嵌入技能制品内部的类型化权威层（而非像工作流引擎那样置于工具之间），包含对世界状态和传感器证据的防护检查。在实时边缘控制平台测试床上部署，针对5种借用权威攻击变体进行评估，测试拦截效果与良性请求放行率，并验证5倍规模扩展性及跨主机Tailscale组网下的鲁棒性。

Result: Edge Skillguard在全部5种攻击变体中以60/60的完美拦截率拒绝借用权威请求，且未阻断任何良性请求。该结果在5倍负载放大及跨主机Tailscale网格部署条件下保持不变，表明将类型化调用策略与程序性知识共同打包进高风险技能，可使物理动作依赖机器可验证证据而非对等代理声明。

Conclusion: 高风险技能应内置类型化调用策略与程序性知识，通过机器可校验的世界状态与传感器证据授权物理动作，而非依赖代理间的权限声明。Edge Skillguard证明了在技能制品层面植入证据型防护可有效封堵'借用权威'攻击链，为具身代理安全提供了可落地的工程范式。

Abstract: Self-evolving Skill harnesses (AutoSkills, Hermes Agent) generate more advisory orchestration automatically; their reported gains are efficiency, not safety. This misses the actual gap: a Skill describes how an agent should behave; a Policy decides which behavior is allowed to become an action. Today's format covers the first with markdown and scripts; the second is left to the model. Generating more Skills scales the gap, not the safety, especially when a wrong invocation can unlock a door or move money. Two adjacent attacks are documented: malicious skills compromising cloud software, and jailbroken LLM-controlled robots causing physical harm. Their intersection, malicious agent skills causing physical harm, follows directly but has not been reported. We name this class Borrowed Authority: Skills format gives the receiving agent no typed way to reject an inter-agent permission claim, so a malicious or misused Skill can drive actuation by attaching one. We propose Edge Skillguard, a typed authority layer that lives inside the Skill artifact rather than between tools as workflow engines do, with guards over world state and sensor evidence. On a live edge control-plane testbed, the guards reject 60/60 borrowed-authority requests across five attack variants without blocking benign requests, and the result holds at 5x scale and across hosts over a Tailscale mesh. These results suggest that high-risk Skills should co-package typed invocation policy with procedural knowledge, so that physical actions depend on machine-checkable evidence rather than peer-agent claims.

</details>


### [208] [PhysElite: How Far Are LLMs from Solving Olympiad-Level Physics Problems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25097)
*Ruoran Xu,Wending Gao,Liyunfeng Chen,Aixin Shi,Haoyu Cheng,Zixiang Fang,Yiqiang Zou,Qiufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出PhysElite，一个大规模双语多模态奥林匹克级物理推理基准，包含11,586道奥赛级物理题，配有视觉图表、逐步中英双语解题推导和最终答案。评测18个开源/闭源MLLMs，最强模型仅达33.7%准确率，并通过步骤级过程评估诊断推理链失败点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理基准存在两大局限：(1) 缺乏高难度数据集，(2) 缺乏视觉形式、知识点和逐步解题过程的全面覆盖，导致模型在当前数据集上的表现不能完全代表其解决复杂物理问题的能力。

Method: 构建PhysElite基准：收集11,586道奥林匹克级物理问题，提供对应视觉图表、逐步中英双语解题推导和最终答案。对18个开源和闭源多模态大语言模型进行基准测试，并进行步骤级过程评估以诊断推理链中的失败点。

Result: 即使最强模型在PhysElite上的答案准确率也仅达到33.7%，表明当前MLLMs在奥赛级物理推理上表现较差。步骤级评估揭示了模型在推理链中的具体失败位置。数据集已发布在Hugging Face。

Conclusion: PhysElite填补了高难度、全覆盖、多模态物理推理基准的空白，揭示了当前MLLMs在复杂物理问题求解上的显著不足，为未来模型改进提供了诊断依据和评测标准。

Abstract: Understanding how (multimodal) large language models perform on physics problems requires benchmarks that reflect the difficulty and breadth of expert-level physical reasoning. Existing physics benchmarks remain limited in the following two important ways: (1) short of high-difficulty datasets, and (2) lack of comprehensive coverage of visual forms, knowledge points, and step-by-step solution processes. As a result, model performance on current datasets may not be fully representative of their ability to solve complex physics problems. To address these issues, we present PhysElite, a large-scale bilingual multimodal benchmark for Olympiad-level physics reasoning. PhysElite contains 11,586 Olympiad-tier problems. For each problem, we provide corresponding visual diagrams, step-by-step bilingual Chinese-English solution derivations, and the final answer. We benchmark 18 open-source and closed-source MLLMs, and find that even the strongest model reaches only 33.7% answer accuracy. We additionally conduct step-level process evaluation to diagnose where models fail in the reasoning chain. Our datasets are released at https://huggingface.co/datasets/physelite/PhysElite.

</details>


### [209] [Towards Reliable, Generalizable, and Specific In-Context Knowledge Editing via Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25100)
*Xuzhong Wang,Maiqi Jiang,Tejal Nair,Girija Bhusal,Yanfu Zhang,Haipeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MO-IKE，一种多目标强化学习算法，将上下文知识编辑的提示构建建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程，动态优化可靠性、泛化性和特异性三个竞争目标，在 Llama-3.2 上显著提升编辑成功率、改写一致性及保留率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的静态参数知识随预训练结束而过时，知识编辑旨在无需全量重训练即更新模型行为。上下文知识编辑因无需训练且适用于黑盒模型而受关注。现有基于强化学习的方法虽改进了检索策略，但未将提示建模为结构化实体，且多仅优化单一目标、仅决策部分提示构建过程，忽视了可靠性、泛化性、特异性多目标平衡及演示的全局组织。

Method: 提出 MO-IKE（多目标上下文知识编辑），将提示构建建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP），训练动态检索器联合优化可靠性、泛化性、特异性三个竞争目标，实现更平衡且全局连贯的提示构建。

Result: 在 Llama-3.2 上，MO-IKE 将编辑成功率（可靠性）从 85.0% 提升至 92.0%，改写一致性（泛化性）从 77% 提升至 79%，保留率（特异性）较先前基于 RL 的方法提高 23.0%。

Conclusion: MO-IKE 通过多目标强化学习实现了更平衡的提示构建，显著提升了知识编辑的可靠性、泛化性和特异性，有效解决了现有方法忽视多目标平衡和全局组织的问题。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are powerful but limited by static parametric knowledge that becomes outdated once pretraining ends. Knowledge editing addresses this problem by updating model behavior on target facts without full retraining. In particular, in-context knowledge editing has gained attention because it is training-free and readily applicable to black-box LLMs. Recent reinforcement learning (RL)-based approaches improve over fixed retrieval strategies by adapting prompt construction to the quantity-quality trade-off. Despite initial success, they fail to model the prompt as a structured entity under the distinct and often competing objectives of reliability, generality, and specificity. Previous methods largely optimize a single objective and make decisions over only part of the prompt construction process, thereby overlooking both the balance of different objectives and the global organization of demonstrations. We propose Multi-Objective In-context Knowledge Editing (MO-IKE), a multi-objective RL algorithm that formulates prompt construction for in-context knowledge editing as a Constrained Markov Decision Process. MO-IKE trains a dynamic retriever to optimize competing objectives in knowledge editing, enabling more balanced and globally coherent prompt construction. On Llama-3.2, MO-IKE improves edit success (reliability) from 85.0% to 92.0%, paraphrase consistency (generality) from 77% to 79%, while increasing retention rate (specificity) by 23.0% compared to prior RL-based methods.

</details>


### [210] [FuzzingBrain-Bench V1: Evaluating Open-Ended Bug Discovery by LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25158)
*Ze Sheng,Aleksandar Kezic,Zhicheng Chen,Jeff Huang*

Main category: cs.AI
